#### How do I get started with your agency?

Getting started is easy! Simply reach out to us through our contact form, phone, or email, and we'll schedule an initial consultation to discuss your project requirements.

#### What services do you offer?

We provide a range of services including web design, graphic design, digital marketing, branding, and more.

#### Does your agency offer free support?

Yes, we offer complimentary support for a period of 30 days following the completion and launch of your project. This support is designed to address any technical issues or bugs that may arise after the project goes live.

#### Do Web Ecommmerce Pros offer consulting services?

Yes, we offer consulting services to help clients with strategic planning, digital transformation, and other initiatives to enhance their online presence and business objectives.

#### What type of support and maintenance do you offer post-project completion?

We provide ongoing support and maintenance services to ensure our clients' websites and digital assets remain up-to-date, secure, and optimized for performance.

#### What Ecommerce stores & platforms can you service?

We have experience working with 100’s of different ecommerce websites and shopping carts. Some of the most common requests come from clients that use: Shopify, BigCommerce, Volusion, Magento, WooCommerce, WordPress, OpenCart, Shift4Shop and Zen Cart.

Asked

#### How long before we see results?

More than 200 different ranking signals go into determining how your site will rank for your targeted keywords, so it’s hard to precisely predict how long results will take. SEO is best thought of as a long-term strategy. However, we can tell you that our clients generally see a marked improvement on long tail keywords in the first 3 months and on more competitive keywords in about 180 days.

#### What changes will my website need, if any?

Most websites or stores don’t need changes to the structure or design for optimization, just changes to the content or code of individual products and pages. Many clients decide to have Web Ecommerce Pros staff make these changes, but we can also provide directives to your staff if you’d rather make these changes internally.

#### What Ecommerce stores & platforms can you service?

We have experience working with 100’s of different ecommerce websites and shopping carts. Some of the most common requests come from clients that use: Shopify, BigCommerce, Volusion, Magento, WooCommerce, WordPress, OpenCart, 3dCart and Zen Cart.

#### What is the cost for an Ecommerce SEO package?

888-711-2347

#### Are there any guarantees?

Any SEO company that promises specific rankings can’t be telling the whole truth, according to Google. Why? Because the Google algorithm changes so often. Therefore, we cannot guarantee rankings, but we do have a commitment to quality work and an established track record of achieving live, measurable results for our clients. Your success is our success!

Social Media

We take the headaches and hassles out of running your social media accounts…so you can get back to running your business! Having an active presence on social media has become necessary to maintain your position in the marketplace and stay ahead of your competition. To get started, please fill out the form or call us to see how we can help you to grow.

Competition

Having an active social media presence can’t be ignored these days…because your competition will engage with your customers and prospects using social media if you don’t! Even if you operate a business that doesn’t have a lot of “social” interaction, you still need to maintain one or more social profiles to give signals within the marketplace that your business is real, legitimate and openly communicates with its customers and prospects. A social presence also plays an integral part in SEO (search engine optimization) as Google relies more and more on signals from social media accounts as a means to gauge your business activity against your competition.

### Let's Migrate

Unlock new possibilities with hassle-free migration.

888-711-2347

Migration

#### Feeling like it’s time to move your online store?

Perhaps your store has expanded and you have outgrown your current shopping cart platform ….maybe your current ecommerce system just doesn’t have the features you need to grow your business…maybe it’s the fees or expense of your current provider…no matter what the reasons may be…

#### We can help you move your store easily, with fewer headaches and no downtime and no loss of sales or revenue.

It may seem like a daunting task to migrate your store to a new ecommerce platform…once you take into account all of the important design, product, customer and order data…and while it is a big technical undertaking with lots of details involved…

#### We have successfully managed the process for dozens of store owners and we can help you!

You can rely on our vast experience working with all major shopping cart systems to help you make your move quickly and efficiently.

We are here to help you transfer your store and data and to ensure nothing gets lost in the process; you will be up and running with your new ecommerce platform easier and faster than you ever imagined.

We offer online store and shopping cart migration services along with ecommerce data entry services at very cost-effective prices…with some of the best turnaround times in the industry.

#### How We Plan & Manage Moving Your Ecommerce Store & Data

We work closely with you to thoroughly plan the entire migration process, so each and every element of your store is accounted for and to ensure nothing is lost during migration.

Along with you, we plan precise dates and a timetable to execute the store transfer process to ensure you have little to no downtime and no interruption of sales.

We study carefully…then devise a plan specifically for your business, how to best organize your store design, products, customers and order data.

We devise the most efficient plan to map all of the key elements including any existing design and product and category pages to the new eCommerce platform you have decided to move to.

We automate the process as much as possible relying on bulk data exports to move your entire product database with carefully mapped designs and product data. This results in less time for your project keeping your costs low.

We perform a full site SEO audit to map your existing store URLs before the move to account for every page that is currently visible and ranking in the search engines. This step is key to ensuring that you don’t suffer any significant drop in organic rankings and traffic to your store website.

Once the move is complete, before going live, we carefully cross check and re-check all pages and functionality to avoid surprises after the move.

We offer you peace of mind and support in the days after the move, knowing we’ll be here to assist if you discover any glitches or corrections that need to be made.

#### How do I get started with your agency?

Getting started is easy! Simply reach out to us through our contact form, phone, or email, and we'll schedule an initial consultation to discuss your project requirements.

We have experience working with 100’s of different ecommerce websites and shopping carts. Some of the most common requests come from clients that use: Shopify, BigCommerce, Volusion, Magento, WooCommerce, WordPress, OpenCart, 3dCart and Zen Cart.

Frequently Asked Questions

#### What services are included in your full-service Amazon account management?

You can get customized services tailored to your needs. Conventionally, our full-service package includes everything from account set-up and optimization to inventory management and conversions.

#### Can you help optimize my PPC campaigns to reduce ACOS?

Yes, our agency performs in-depth keyword research, refines targeting, and adjusts bids based on real-time data. We may implement automated and manual bidding strategies and continually monitor and tweak campaigns to lower your TACoS & ACoS. Our strategies are focused on long-term growth and organic rankings.

#### How can we help you with brand protection and intellectual property rights?

Our VAs monitor your listings using software tools to detect unauthorized sellers. They then act by filing infringement complaints through Amazon’s Brand Registry or forwarding the issue to Amazon’s legal team to remove the hijacker from your listing.

#### How do you report on progress and results?

We provide weekly and monthly reports regarding sales metrics, inventory status, customer feedback, ad performance, and more. We also schedule regular calls to discuss concerns, progress, and possible growth opportunities.

#### Do you assist in customer services and feedback management?

Yes, we actively monitor customers' feedback. Our team responds to customers' inquiries on time, providing a positive shopping experience. We also help increase product reviews by following Amazon’s guidelines for requesting reviews through Amazon’s request-a-review button. For negative feedback, we use Amazon’s Feedback Manager to resolve and possibly remove negative reviews.

#### How can you help launch new products successfully on Amazon?

In summary, our steps involve optimized listings, strategic keyword placement, and a targeted PPC campaign. If needed, we might also use targeted promotions, limited-time discounts, and social media campaigns to build initial momentum for higher ranks in the marketplace.

#### How do you manage inventory and FBA logistics?

We utilize advanced inventory management tools to track available stock and forecast demand. Our team monitors your FBA logistics to comply with Amazon’s requirements and minimize the risks of overstock or stockout situations. We also track reimbursements and ensure you’re compensated based on Amazon’s policies.

#### What level of access will I have to my account?

You’ll retain full access to your account. In case of safety concerns, you can get customized services by sharing child access with us. We also provide insights and updates through shared reports and communication to keep you in the loop regarding your business performance.

#### Do you offer international account management services for selling on multiple Amazon marketplaces?

Yes, our agency helps manage your global Amazon accounts by ensuring compliance with the different marketplace regulations. We handle VAT registration, currency conversion, and inventory management across international fulfillment centers.

#### How much do you charge?

Our pricing model is straightforward and transparent. You can request a free audit and get a quote for required services. Whether you're looking for a flat monthly feeor pay-per-task, we can customize a package according to your budget and goals.

### AI SEO

Your online store no longer needs to guess what Google cares about; you will be ahead of it. With AI SEO, we don’t just optimise your store for today’s search algorithms. We train your site to adapt to tomorrow. By ruling large language models, natural-language understanding, and predictive analytics, your brand becomes the answer people receive when they ask their next question to AI. So stop chasing rankings and start orchestrating visibility.

### AI Visibility Service

Clear information leads to more visibility in AI search engines. So, we start by tightening the structure of your site. Product data and attributes. We’ll clean up your schema and standardize it so AI can read your pages without confusion.Then we’ll work on your content if needed. Your content needs to be answer-focused to get a slot in LLM responses. Short explanations, direct Q&A sections, and clear comparisons give AI the exact details it needs to pull your products into responses. We also refine how your pages communicate intent. Strong topic alignment, consistent metadata, and well-defined product details help AI match your store to the questions real customers are asking (fan-out query). Finally, we ensure AI can actually access your content via clean URLs, correct robots.txt settings, and accurate sitemaps that point crawlers to the right pages.

### Benifits for Ecommerce Stores

Unlock deeper shopper intent, automate SEO at scale, future-proof your store for AI-driven search, and build stronger trust with customers through clear, modern, answer-focused content.

#### Auto-Optimisation at Scale

#### Isn’t this just regular SEO with a new name?

No, as regular SEO focuses on keywords, links, and ranking positions, whereas AI SEO focuses on optimising for AI-driven results, voice assistants, generative responses, and evolving search behaviour.

#### How does this help my ecommerce store?

In your niche, you’ll surface for conversations. That difference drives higher-intent traffic and better conversions.

#### Do I need to replace all my existing SEO work?

No. AI SEO is the evolution of it. Your foundational SEO (site speed, links, on-page) matters; we build on top of that so you’re visible in the newer AI-driven discovery systems.

#### Will this cost more than our usual SEO budget?

It may require a recalibrated investment initially because the tools, data, and strategy differ. But the ROI is amplified when you also capture modern AI-driven traffic channels others aren’t ready for.

Dedicated Amazon

Hiring a dedicated Amazon Ads expert provides a level of focus and customization that shared resources can’t match. With a dedicated expert, you have someone solely committed to your campaigns, giving them their full attention and adapting strategies specifically to your business goals. Unlike shared teams who manage multiple clients, a dedicated expert can respond more quickly to changes, optimize campaigns in real-time, and deliver deeper insights. They take the time to understand your brand, products, and competition, ensuring your ad spend is used efficiently to maximize results. This personalized approach leads to more consistent, impactful performance compared to a shared service.

Advertising

We’ve streamlined the process of hiring a dedicated Amazon Ads expert to make it as smooth and straightforward as possible. Here’s how it works:

1

#### Initial Consultation

We start with a free consultation to understand your business needs, advertising goals, and challenges. This helps us tailor our services specifically to your brand.

Let's Start

2

#### Expert Matching

Based on your goals and business requirements, we match you with the right Amazon Ads expert who specializes in your industry and advertising objectives.

3

#### Proposal & Agreement

Once you've approved the match, we present a clear proposal outlining the strategy, services, and pricing. We ensure transparency with no hidden fees, and we establish a Non-Disclosure Agreement (NDA) to protect your business information.

4

#### Onboarding & Strategy Development

After agreement, the expert works closely with you to develop a customized Amazon Ads strategy, ensuring alignment with your business vision. This includes account access setup, reporting preferences, and key performance indicators (KPIs).

5

#### Launch & Ongoing Management

Once the campaigns are live, the dedicated expert continuously manages, optimizes, and provides detailed performance reports, while keeping you informed with regular updates.

6

#### Flexible Exit Option

If your needs change, we offer an easy exit process with no long-term commitments or cancellation fees, ensuring flexibility throughout the partnership.

We understand that every business is unique, which is why we offer tailored strategies that align with your specific goals, ensuring long-term success on Amazon. Backed by a proven track record of successful Vendor Central management, we’ve helped businesses of all sizes optimize operations, boost sales, and build lasting customer relationships.

Process

Our streamlined approach ensures that every aspect of your Amazon Vendor Central account is managed efficiently and effectively. We provide a comprehensive solution tailored to your unique business needs.

1

#### Initial Consultation & Assessment

We start by understanding your business needs and analyzing your existing Vendor Central setup to identify areas for improvement.

Let's Start

#### Review & Feedback Management

#### Performance Reporting

We provide regular, in-depth reports that offer insights into your account’s performance, sales trends, and advertising effectiveness, allowing you to make informed decisions.

We had an excellent experience working with Web Commerce Pros on the migration of our shopping cart from Volusion to Shopify. The transition was smooth and well-managed from start to finish. Their team was highly responsive, attentive to detail, and consistently available to answer questions and provide guidance throughout the process. They not only handled the technical migration efficiently, but also did an outstanding job redesigning our website to match our specific vision and requirements. We are very pleased with the final result and would confidently recommend Web Commerce Pros to any company looking for a professional, reliable partner for e-commerce development and platform migration.

hemptraders.com

F

We worked with Web Ecommerce Pros on a multi-month migration from Volusion to Shopify Plus for our retail website. The project involved complex product structures, digital downloads, discount logic, integrations, and a tightly coordinated launch. As with any migration of this size, requirements evolved as we moved from planning into execution. Roy and his team stayed engaged, responsive, and solutions-oriented throughout the process. When issues surfaced, they worked through them methodically and kept the project moving forward. The technical execution was solid, the launch was successful, and the site is performing well post-go-live. Web Ecommerce Pros is a strong partner for organizations that understand their requirements, stay involved, and want a team that can handle real-world complexity rather than a cookie-cutter implementation.

fabriccafe.com

S

When I started my e-commerce company in the early 2000s, I had to set up the website by myself. It was a very frustrating experience for someone who knew nothing about website design and code, it was a very steep and painful learning curve. Several years later, I transitioned to Volusion, which had an awesome support team at the time, and I also hired a really good graphic artist to help me with a Volusion refresh some time after that; but overall, it's been me by myself trying to manage website design and troubleshooting. Because Volusion support went downhill over the past 2-3 years, I recently decided it was finally time to leave Volusion, and I hired WEP to migrate my store to Shopify. I am so relieved that I have finally crossed paths with an honest, responsible, and competent group of professionals whom I feel I can turn to anytime I need website help. I try to do as much as I can on my own, which is not a bad thing, but there are times when I get stuck or I just don't have the time to deal with it; so, I'm glad I now have a trusted company I can turn to as a partner. If I need any future website support, I'll be sure to reach out to Roy at WEP.

splashgearusa.com

I had a very positive experience working with Roy and his team at Web Ecommerce Pros. Roy offered superb communication and was a great problem solver when encountering all the twists and turns that arise when doing a large site migration. I love the new site design, improved functionality, and am very proud of the end result. I would work with them again and wouldn't hesitate to recommend them to others.

futurememories.com

C

I had Volusion for 15 years as my host and it was never a very good experience. i have wanted to move to another provider and i finally did that using Ecommerce pros. It was a great experience. They delivered as promised. They even did some things that i wanted done that weren't really in the quote and they didn't charge for that. Definitely would use them again!!

I continue to enjoy working with this company. They created the most beautiful website ever! They are proactive and helped get my product off the ground quickly. Their knowledge in navigating the backend of Shopify as well as social media has been a huge help. I trust them implicitly and bonus they are fun to collaborate with.

E

We were in desperate need to move from Volusion to Big commerce as Volusion was not keeping up with all the available apps and Big commerce is a much user friendly platform for a small business like ours. I was really worried about the cost but found Web Ecommerce Pros and it was the best decision we made. They gave us a reasonable price, one that we could afford to do the transition from Volusion to Big Commerce and their service was outstanding. Very responsive, listened to our needs and we were able to move to Big commerce and have our website up and running with no issue. Even after a week of having our website live if there were some glitches on a link or image etc they were still helping to fix those kinks. Highly recommend. They are a very professional team. Asked

#### How do I get started with your agency?

Getting started is easy! Simply reach out to us through our contact form, phone, or email, and we'll schedule an initial consultation to discuss your project requirements.

#### What services do you offer?

We provide a range of services including web design, graphic design, digital marketing, branding, and more.

#### Does your agency offer free support?

Yes, we offer complimentary support for a period of 30 days following the completion and launch of your project. This support is designed to address any technical issues or bugs that may arise after the project goes live.

#### Do Web Ecommmerce Pros offer consulting services?

Yes, we offer consulting services to help clients with strategic planning, digital transformation, and other initiatives to enhance their online presence and business objectives.

#### What type of support and maintenance do you offer post-project completion?

We provide ongoing support and maintenance services to ensure our clients' websites and digital assets remain up-to-date, secure, and optimized for performance.

#### What Ecommerce stores & platforms can you service?

We have experience working with 100’s of different ecommerce websites and shopping carts. Some of the most common requests come from clients that use: Shopify, BigCommerce, Volusion, Magento, WooCommerce, WordPress, OpenCart, 3dCart and Zen Cart.

### Let's Migrate

Unlock new possibilities with hassle-free migration.

888-711-2347

Migration

#### Feeling like it’s time to move your online store?

Perhaps your store has expanded and you have outgrown your current shopping cart platform ….maybe your current ecommerce system just doesn’t have the features you need to grow your business…maybe it’s the fees or expense of your current provider…no matter what the reasons may be…

#### We can help you move your store easily, with fewer headaches and no downtime and no loss of sales or revenue.

It may seem like a daunting task to migrate your store to a new ecommerce platform…once you take into account all of the important design, product, customer and order data…and while it is a big technical undertaking with lots of details involved…

#### We have successfully managed the process for dozens of store owners and we can help you!

You can rely on our vast experience working with all major shopping cart systems to help you make your move quickly and efficiently.

We are here to help you transfer your store and data and to ensure nothing gets lost in the process; you will be up and running with your new ecommerce platform easier and faster than you ever imagined.

We offer online store and shopping cart migration services along with ecommerce data entry services at very cost-effective prices…with some of the best turnaround times in the industry.

#### How We Plan & Manage Moving Your Ecommerce Store & Data

We work closely with you to thoroughly plan the entire migration process, so each and every element of your store is accounted for and to ensure nothing is lost during migration.

Along with you, we plan precise dates and a timetable to execute the store transfer process to ensure you have little to no downtime and no interruption of sales.

We study carefully…then devise a plan specifically for your business, how to best organize your store design, products, customers and order data.

We devise the most efficient plan to map all of the key elements including any existing design and product and category pages to the new eCommerce platform you have decided to move to.

We automate the process as much as possible relying on bulk data exports to move your entire product database with carefully mapped designs and product data. This results in less time for your project keeping your costs low.

We perform a full site SEO audit to map your existing store URLs before the move to account for every page that is currently visible and ranking in the search engines. This step is key to ensuring that you don’t suffer any significant drop in organic rankings and traffic to your store website.

Once the move is complete, before going live, we carefully cross check and re-check all pages and functionality to avoid surprises after the move.

We offer you peace of mind and support in the days after the move, knowing we’ll be here to assist if you discover any glitches or corrections that need to be made.

Asked

#### How long before we see results?

More than 200 different ranking signals go into determining how your site will rank for your targeted keywords, so it’s hard to precisely predict how long results will take. SEO is best thought of as a long-term strategy. However, we can tell you that our clients generally see a marked improvement on long tail keywords in the first 3 months and on more competitive keywords in about 180 days.

#### What changes will my website need, if any?

Most websites or stores don’t need changes to the structure or design for optimization, just changes to the content or code of individual products and pages. Many clients decide to have Web Ecommerce Pros staff make these changes, but we can also provide directives to your staff if you’d rather make these changes internally.

#### What Ecommerce stores & platforms can you service?

We have experience working with 100’s of different ecommerce websites and shopping carts. Some of the most common requests come from clients that use: Shopify, BigCommerce, Volusion, Magento, WooCommerce, WordPress, OpenCart, 3dCart and Zen Cart.

#### What is the cost for an Ecommerce SEO package?

888-711-2347

#### Are there any guarantees?

Any SEO company that promises specific rankings can’t be telling the whole truth, according to Google. Why? Because the Google algorithm changes so often. Therefore, we cannot guarantee rankings, but we do have a commitment to quality work and an established track record of achieving live, measurable results for our clients. Your success is our success!

## SEO Considerations for Ecommerce Migration

Jack Mathew

Updated on Nov 24, 2025

When you move your store from one platform to another, your SEO is riding shotgun with your revenue. Done right, you carry all your rankings and traffic with you. Done wrong, you’re back at square one, fighting to win back visibility you already earned.

preserve traffic & avoid surprises.

A solid SEO migration keeps that in focus, so your visibility and revenue stay stable while you upgrade. This guide explains the SEO steps that help protect your traffic and sales during migration. So, let’s get some insights:

### Why SEO Matters So Much During a Migration

Whether you’re shifting from legacy platforms to modern platforms, switching from one modern ecommerce platform to another contemporary option, or redesigning your existing store, your new site must signal to Google that it’s still the same trusted business.

Every product page, collection, and blog post is like a savings account where you’ve stored SEO equity over time. That equity comes from backlinks, internal links, and Google’s confidence in your content.

If those connections get lost during the move, it’s as if the accounts were wiped clean, and Google will treat your store like it’s brand new.

well-planned migration

### Core SEO Principles During Migration That Never Change

Some SEO rules apply to every migration, no matter which platform you’re moving to. These are the non-negotiables.

Use 301 Redirects, Not Shortcuts:

Tell Google About the Move:

Don’t Accidentally Block Crawlers:

Trust Platform Canonicals:

Expect a Dip:

### Pre-Migration Discovery

Before you touch anything, you need a complete picture of your current site. This is your safety net, and this is the foundation your redirect strategy will rest on. Skipping it means flying blind.

What to include in your discovery:

A full list of URLs — products, categories, blogs, filters, images.

A priority list of “money” pages with top traffic, top revenue, and top-linked URLs.

A review of filter and pagination parameters to decide what to keep.

Baselines for site performance, mobile parity, schema, and canonicals.

An inventory of internal and external links that need protection.

### Redirect Strategy That Holds Rankings

Redirects are the glue that carries your SEO equity across platforms. Get them wrong, and your site can bleed traffic from day one.

A strong redirect strategy includes:

1:1 mapping with single-hop 301s — no chains, no shortcuts.

Covering media files like images and thumbnails when possible, since image search often drives hidden traffic.

Testing in staging using a crawler to confirm every redirect and canonical is correct before launch.

### Post-Migration Monitoring

The launch isn’t the end of the process; in fact, it’s the start of your watch period. The first 4 to 6 weeks are critical. Focus on these areas as quick fixes in this phase often determine how quickly rankings rebound.:

Search Console:

404 logs:

Sitemaps:

Analytics:

### Platform-Specific SEO Considerations

SEO of the website

#### Shopify

Shopify’s structure is opinionated: products live under /products/, collections under /collections/, and blogs under /blogs/. You can change handles, but not these core folders, so redirect maps must reflect that.

Shopify also injects canonical tags automatically — don’t double up unless you know what you’re doing. Watch out for duplicate content from product variants, tags, and pagination. And if you’re using Shopify Markets, avoid mass duplication across regions; use canonicals or localize content.

#### BigCommerce

BigCommerce gives more flexibility with URL structures and has a built-in 301 Redirect Manager (with an API for bulk work). Internal links should always be updated — don’t rely only on redirects. And check canonical settings to prevent duplicate paths from being indexed.

#### Turbify (Yahoo Store)

Older Yahoo/Turbify stores often have quirky URL patterns. Mapping those to Shopify or BigCommerce requires more custom redirect work. The golden rule here is: pre-map every path before launch. Missing even a handful of high-value pages can cost dearly.

#### Volusion

Volusion migrations often involve slug changes. Preserve SEO-friendly slugs when possible, and use 301s for any that must change. BigCommerce’s API can bulk upload redirects to handle larger catalogs efficiently.

### Content, Structure, and Internal Linking

Migrating isn’t just about URLs; it’s about carrying forward the content that makes you visible.

Keep the on-page copy, reviews, FAQs, and H1s that already rank. Don’t throw out long-tail category pages if they’ve been working. Update all internal links to point to final URLs, not redirects, so Google has a clean path to follow.

#### International and Multi-Market Stores

If you sell internationally, your SEO challenges multiply. A careless migration can wipe out hreflang signals and duplicate content.

The key rules are:

Preserve existing hreflang tags during the move.

Shopify Markets

For ccTLD-to-subfolder moves, treat each market like its own mini-migration with unique mapping and QA.

### Performance, UX, and Structured Data

Migration is also a chance to check site speed and structured data. A new theme doesn’t guarantee a faster site — sometimes it’s slower. Test Core Web Vitals before and after launch.

Don’t forget images. Redirect old image URLs and preserve alt text if image search brings traffic. Structured data should also survive the move: product details, breadcrumbs, and organization schema all help rankings and click-through rates.

### Tips from Developers & SEO Experts

With over 2 decades in this industry, we have listed a few of the most important experts' tips that even most store owners who’ve been through a migration vouch for:

“301s are the #1 issue.”

“Expect a dip, but plan for recovery.”

“Don’t fight the platform’s canonicals.”

“Test before launch.”

Note:

### Quick Migration Checklist

Meticulously do all that and then your store can keep its rankings, protect sales, and even come out stronger on the other side.

Before Launch

Crawl and export all URLs.

Build a 301 map for every page that matters.

Copy metadata, H1s, and schema.

Test staging for noindex and broken links.

Verify both domains in Google Search Console.

Launch Day

Activate 301s.

Submit fresh sitemaps.

File Change of Address in GSC (if domain changes).

Spot-check top pages for 200 status and the right content.

After Launch

Monitor 404s daily.

Watch GSC for redirect or canonical errors.

Replace old internal links.

Track traffic and revenue closely.

### Key Takeaways

Redirects make or break migrations. Every old URL needs a one-to-one 301 pointing to the right new page. Anything less risks traffic loss.

Tell Google what’s happening. Use Search Console’s Change of Address tool for domain moves and submit clean new sitemaps.

Preserve what already works. Titles, H1s, product copy, reviews, FAQs, and structured data should move with you.

Don’t fight the platform. Shopify and BigCommerce handle canonicals and URL patterns in specific ways — plan within those guardrails.

Expect a small dip. Even well-executed migrations can dip briefly; the difference between a blip and a crash is how well you plan.

Monitor closely post-launch. Daily 404 checks, Search Console alerts, and quick fixes keep rankings steady while Google adjusts.

### How Web Ecommerce Pros Can Help You

Migrating a well-established ecommerce store in itself is an overwhelming task. You've got to be careful, or else you’ll have to get back in a fight with your competitors to secure a place on SERP from scratch.

In all honesty, if your store has customization, it’s best to ask for help from industry experts instead of relying on automated migration tools and risking your years' worth of efforts.

At Web Ecommerce Pros, we have been helping ecommerce merchants since 2005. Whether you need to migrate your store to a modern platform or require tailored solutions for migration or within your existing store, we can assist you with our 20+ years of experience in a multitude of migration, development, and management across diverse ecommerce platforms and businesses.

reach out to us

## Retargeting Ads: A cost-effective way to recover lost sales

Jack Mathew

Updated on Oct 31, 2025

Running an e-commerce business is thrilling until sales stall.

You invest in ads, build a presence across every platform, and see traffic arrive. People browse, even add to cart, but don’t buy. And being an online merchant, you can only watch your hard-earned clicks drift away, unlike a brick-and-mortar shop where you can walk over, answer questions, and flip the script to keep customers from leaving.

In this scenario, guessing why they left rarely works. Hoping they’ll return on their own almost never does. The smartest move is to intervene and revive that halted buying decision while the intent is still warm.

The best and most cost-effective way to revive halted buying intent is to re-engage those very people who browsed, considered, or abandoned their carts and do it before they disappear for good.

This is exactly what retargeting ads do: lower your cost per acquisition, and reclaim 30–40% of sales that would otherwise vanish.

### Retargeting vs Remarketing vs Prospecting

First, let’s clear the possible confusion between retargeting vs prospecting ads.

Prospecting or cold ads

Retargeting

Remarketing

Knowing these distinctions helps when planning campaigns: Use retargeting for paid ads and remarketing for owned channels like email or SMS. Together, they create a powerful recovery system.

### Why Retargeting Works

e-commerce websites

Each theory adds another layer of anxiety and rarely leads to clarity.

Amidst all this confusion, the shoppers you worked so hard to attract drift away, their interest cools down by the hour.

Now, let’s quickly address the main reason why retargeting ads are not a choice; it's a necessity.

Only a few shoppers buy at first sight; however, that does not mean the other won’t buy from you at all. As per the common shopping norms, your customers tend to first compare prices, weigh trust, hesitate over shipping, or simply get distracted.

And in the meantime, your competitors are also trying to get them with smarter ads and follow-up that will quietly reclaim your potential traffic.

However, this won’t happen with the right retargeting strategy in place from the get-go. You can bring many of those visitors back at a fraction of the cost of acquiring brand-new traffic.

Retargeting ads reintroduce your brand to those customers who are already interested in your products. The key is knowing how to set them up strategically, not just turn them on and hope.

This guide will walk you through everything — from understanding retargeting psychology to building high-performing campaigns across platforms.

### What Retargeting Means in Digital Marketing

digital marketing

Instead of advertising to strangers and hoping they’re interested, retargeting focuses on those who’ve shown real intent. This precision is why it’s considered one of the most cost-effective and ROI-friendly tactics in the digital marketer’s toolkit.

### Essentials for High-Impact Retargeting Campaigns

#### Segment Your Audiences

Retargeting is not about being more louder; it’s about speaking intelligently to someone who’s already listening. It’s cost-efficient because you’re not paying to introduce your brand — you’re reigniting interest that already existed.

The reason it is cost-effective is that you are not persuading strangers; you are re-engaging people who have seen your product and considered it.

Because of this familiarity, retargeting ads routinely outperform cold campaigns in click-through rate and conversion while costing less per acquisition.

First, build a case for your retargeting ad. Not every visitor deserves the same message. Someone who glanced at a product page is different from someone who abandoned a full cart. Past buyers require yet another approach.

Segment audiences by behavior: product viewers, cart abandoners, returning customers, and casual content readers. Doing so allows you to craft messages that match intent. Generic ads feel wasteful; tailored ones feel personal and persuasive.

#### Choose Channels Strategically

After segmentation comes placement.

Facebook and Instagram excel for visually rich consumer products and allow dynamic product ads that show shoppers the exact items they viewed. Google’s Display Network keeps your brand visible as users read articles or browse forums. YouTube works well for products that benefit from storytelling or demonstration.

TikTok and Pinterest thrive where inspiration drives impulse, especially in fashion and lifestyle categories. Start with the channel where your traffic is already strongest, then expand once the first campaigns prove profitable.

#### Create Ads That Convert

Targeting alone does not close sales; creative execution does.

A casual browser may need reassurance about quality and brand credibility. A cart abandoner often responds to urgency, small incentives, or clear shipping information. Past buyers appreciate exclusivity and early access.

Dynamic content elevates this work by showing each shopper the precise item they considered. Add social proof — ratings, testimonials, user photos — to reduce hesitation. Rotate creative angles: one ad focused on craftsmanship, another on lifestyle fit, another on timely savings. Frequent refresh keeps audiences engaged and prevents fatigue.

#### Time and Pace Your Ads

Cart abandonment

Control frequency carefully. Seeing your ad several times a day can persuade; seeing it dozens of times irritates. Use impression caps and exclude people once they convert. Respectful pacing protects your brand image and keeps costs efficient.

#### Budget Intelligently

Retargeting rarely needs massive spend. Many brands begin with modest daily budgets for each audience and scale once return on ad spend proves healthy. High-intent groups, such as cart abandoners, often justify more aggressive bidding because their conversion cost is low.

Automated bidding tools like “Lowest Cost” on Facebook or “Target CPA” on Google — work well once data accumulates. A practical guideline is to dedicate 10–20% of your total ad budget to retargeting. Done well, that fraction can generate 30–40% of sales.

#### Measure and Refine

Retargeting thrives on data. Install tracking pixels to know who viewed, who added to cart, and who purchased. Monitor click-through rate for creative relevance, cost per acquisition for efficiency, and return on ad spend for profitability.

maintenance purpose

#### Integrate With Other Tactics

Ads alone recover many sales, but integration makes them stronger. Abandoned-cart emails and SMS reminders work well when timed alongside retargeting impressions.

Loyalty programs encourage repeat purchases and referrals. Dynamic discount codes create urgency tailored to each shopper. Once retargeting is profitable, lookalike audiences can expand reach to people who resemble your best customers.

This orchestration — ads plus email, SMS, and loyalty builds a seamless recovery system rather than a single, isolated campaign.

#### Avoid Common Pitfalls

Several mistakes drain retargeting budgets. Treating all visitors identically weakens relevance. Continuing to advertise to customers who already bought wastes spend and irritates them. Allowing the same creative to run for months creates banner blindness. Flooding prospects with excessive impressions breeds annoyance instead of trust.

Awareness of these traps and disciplined campaign settings to avoid them keeps retargeting both efficient and brand-safe.

### Key Insights:

Retargeting efficiently converts visitors who have already shown intent, making each recovered sale cheaper than acquiring new customers.

Segment audiences by behavior, like product viewers, cart abandoners, past buyers, and casual site readers, for more relevant messaging.

Begin on the channel where your audience already engages most (such as Facebook/Instagram, Google Display, or YouTube) before expanding to others.

Match creative to intent: reassurance for browsers, urgency or incentives for cart abandoners, exclusivity for loyal buyers, and always include social proof.

Retarget promptly after a visit or cart abandonment, keep campaign windows short, and cap impressions to avoid fatigue and wasted spend.

Allocate about 10–20% of your ad budget to retargeting; well-run campaigns can return 30–40% of overall sales.

Track performance closely with pixels, watch ROAS and CPA, and refresh ads regularly; treat retargeting as an iterative process.

Combine retargeting with email, SMS, and loyalty programs to create a seamless recovery and retention system.

Avoid overexposing users, serving outdated creatives, or targeting people who have already purchased.

Retargeting maximizes the value of traffic you have already paid for, turning lost attention into profitable conversions.

Web Ecommerce Pros Can Help You

With over 20 years of experience in the ecommerce space and a diverse portfolio of successful brands, we understand exactly what makes online shoppers convert.

More often than not, it’s not your product that’s the problem; it’s the ad strategy. A weak or misaligned retargeting approach can easily lose potential customers who were too close to buying.

## What Modern Ecommerce Brands Do Differently

Jack Mathew

Updated on Sep 30, 2025

Ecommerce has matured. Marketing, SEO, and branding aren’t new anymore—they’re baseline expectations. Every ecommerce merchant is already investing in them.

What helps modern ecommerce brands stand out is how they execute creatively, optimize the journey, and design stores that convert better.

In short, modern ecommerce is about solving customer frustrations in smarter ways, shaping journeys that feel personal, and building value beyond the product itself.

Let’s dig into the ecommerce solutions modern brands are opting for.

### Innovation as Customer Experience

Innovation nowadays is about execution. Brands are adding features that directly improve the customer journey.

Modern brands are rolling out features that feel less like novelties and more like everyday problem-solvers. For instance, pet food companies use personalized feeding guidelines. Lifestyle stores send stock alerts with alternative suggestions. Complex product configurators let customers design exactly what they want.

Small creative touches like these increase engagement and lead to smarter, data-backed business moves. They’re the difference between being memorable and being forgettable.

### AI and Personalization at the Core

Personalization has matured from “you might also like” into deeply tailored journeys. Modern brands use AI to create size profiles, curate complete looks, and serve recommendations that feel human, not algorithmic.

Modern websites

Warby Parker’s AR try-ons that make eyewear decisions and Glossier’s hyper-personal product suggestions based on browsing and buying habits show how AI isn’t just about sales, it’s about confidence and trust, because when customers feel understood, they stick.

### Design That Pulls Its Weight

web design

Cart pages are also a focus. Strong cart design reassures, upsells, and encourages completion. A weak cart, on the other hand, bleeds revenue. And smart brands know the difference.

### Reviews As Growth Tool

Reviews aren’t just social proof anymore. They’re mined for insights that fuel business decisions.

They reveal hidden objections, validate product quality, and point to new opportunities. In many cases, reviews guide merchandising as much as marketing. Basically, shoppers witness transparency via reviews, and brands see them as a growth map now.

Similarly, modern brands are also blending social commerce (shopping directly through social feeds) with community building, fostering loyalty and advocacy. They are also heavily leveraging Influencer and creator partnerships to extend reach and add authenticity.

### Storytelling With Substance

Customers want values they can align with, not just products to buy.

Let’s take Allbirds as an example. It puts sustainability at the center of every sneaker. Or Welly, turning everyday first-aid into something playful and design-driven.

And these are not mere marketing stunts. Studies in consumer psychology show that when customers experience brand self-congruence, that feeling that “this is me too”, it develops a sense of love and attachment for your brand in them. Which naturally leads them to advocate for you.

And when your brand story is immersive and emotionally rich, consumers experience what psychology calls narrative transportation. People who can relate to your narrative get so absorbed that they almost live inside it. They remember it more clearly and passionately share it with others.

In the end, this emotional connection doesn’t just help you save on customer acquisition costs. It turns regular buyers into natural storytellers for your brand.

Read More:

How Modern Ecommerce Stores Use Storytelling for Branding

### Smarter Marketing, Less Dependence

Modern brands aren’t just throwing money at ads; they’re building smarter mixes that stretch reach across online, physical, and social platforms. With ad costs climbing, relying only on Google or Meta is a fast way to burn a budget.

Therefore, brands are balancing paid traffic with organic retargeting. For instance:

Email marketing with clever pop-ups builds lists that can lead to repeat sales through newsletters, product drops, seasonal offers, or personalized recommendations.

Organic content like TikToks, Reels, and UGC acts as free creative ads.

Strong branding ensures repeat buyers don’t need to be reacquired at full cost.

Modern ecommerce brands are constantly finding creative ways to cut reliance on Google ads. Content is doing the heavy lifting for them. Paid is still part of the mix, but it’s no longer the main source of conversions.

### Optimizing for the AI-Overview

AI-generated overviews are quickly becoming the first point of interaction for shoppers. Brands that win citations there gain scale without paying for clicks.

To do this, modern brands write structured content that’s AI-readable—clear descriptions, transparent sourcing details, and FAQs aligned to real questions.

They also push consistent storytelling across channels, reinforce credibility through reviews and UGC, and use schema markup to feed structured signals.

The battleground has shifted. Ranking in search matters, but being chosen by AI matters more.

### Migration to Suitable Platforms

migrate away from legacy

Instead, they’re choosing modern platforms that match their size, needs, and scaling goals. These newer platforms are not only faster and more marketing-friendly but also easier to optimize for performance. In the long run, they cost less and reduce technical debt, which frees teams to focus on growth instead of constant maintenance.

In ecommerce, speed and adaptability are worth more than clinging to the old. That’s why brands are also experimenting with emerging tools like blockchain for direct peer-to-peer payments with stablecoins or cryptocurrencies, as well as composable commerce for greater scalability, transparency, and security.

### Flexible Models and Emerging Tech

Differentiation today isn’t just about what you sell but how you sell it.

For instance, innovative products like functional foods, specialized kits or unique subscription models are what retain customers. Add in flexible payment options and convenience-first experiences, and you’ve got the foundation for long-term retention.

Subscription models in particular are reshaping the rules of loyalty in ecommerce by offering value and convenience. Meanwhile, flexible payments remove friction at checkout, turning “maybe later” into “buy now.”

On the tech side, voice commerce, AI search, and interactive shopping are moving fast from buzzwords to must-haves. The brands experimenting with them early aren’t just chasing trends—they’re securing tomorrow’s advantages today.

### Key Takeaways

Innovation today is about solving customer pain points with creative features.

AI personalization, strong design, and cart optimization drive measurable gains.

Reviews help in both trust and product strategy.

Storytelling and community reduce acquisition costs.

Marketing mixes prioritize owned and organic over paid dependence.

AI-overview visibility is the new frontier for organic reach.

Platform migration enables speed, agility, and scalability.

Testing flexible models and emerging tech future-proofs the business.

### Closing Thought

Recent ecommerce trends in brands emphasize eco-friendly packaging, ethical sourcing, and transparent supply chains, aligned with consumer demand for sustainable and values-driven commerce.

Which implies that modern ecommerce isn’t about doing more marketing—it’s about doing it differently. The winners are those who create experiences that feel personal, brand stories that feel human, and operations that work smarter behind the scenes.

## How AI Search Is Reshaping Marketing & SEO for B2B Tools

Jack Mathew

Updated on Sep 5, 2025

Marketing for B2B tools has always been about one thing: visibility where decision-makers are looking.

Previously, visibility meant ranking in Google search results, but now AI-powered search is changing who gets seen, cited, and trusted.

This isn’t just a usual SEO update; it’s a change in the way buyers discover solutions and vendors. And for B2B platforms and ecommerce tools, the implications are massive.

### Why This Matters for B2B Tools

In the old SEO, your goal was simple: outrank competitors in Google’s search results using keywords, backlinks, and domain authority. And the higher you ranked, the more clicks you captured.

AI search breaks that formula. Instead of sending users through the top ten blue links, the AI summarizes an answer and highlights just a few citations.

Buyers often never scroll past that box. Which means your marketing goal has shifted. It’s no longer about ranking for clicks; it’s about being one of the sources the AI chooses to cite.

Shopify Plus

When an AI-generated answer highlights your competitor and ignores you, you don’t just miss a click — you’re cut out of the shortlist entirely. And in B2B, shortlists decide the deals.

### What’s Really Driving AI Citations

First, we need to debunk the myth. Many marketers assume Google’s generative answers are only pulled from the top-ranked search results. That simply isn’t true.

Independent studies by Originality and Ahref have shown that roughly half of AI Overview citations come from outside the top 100 organic results. In fact, platforms like Reddit and LinkedIn are frequently cited, even when they don’t appear anywhere near the top of the SERP.

A recent Forbes Tech Council article mentioned that LinkedIn now accounts for 13% of Google AI Overview citations. That’s a staggering number if you think about it.

Which means that what Google values in AI-generated answers isn’t just authority in the old sense. It’s clarity, structured explanations, and contextual relevance. In short, rank doesn’t equal citation. You need content that AI feels confident quoting word-for-word.

### How B2B Tools Can Win in the AI Search Era

Winning visibility now means rethinking the way you produce and distribute content.

Start by creating content that is “answer-ready.” Think of the kinds of questions your buyers ask directly, like which platform handles enterprise-level B2B best, how your tool supports predictive churn analytics for B2B buyers, or what certifications your tool holds.

Clear comparisons, structured FAQs, and compliance summaries are exactly the type of material AI prefers to lift, so focus on them.

Then consider verifiable signals. Structured data through JSON-LD, detailed product feeds through services like Feedonomics, and downloadable assets like PDFs or case studies all serve as reliable, citable material for AI systems.

Next, expand your presence to the places AI is actively looking beyond Google. LinkedIn is a powerful example, but professional communities, niche forums, and customer review platforms are also increasingly shaping AI-generated answers. A well-placed testimonial or expert insight can echo far louder than an extra backlink ever did.

Finally, rethink your metrics. Traditional keyword rankings hold value, but they don’t tell the full story anymore. You should be tracking whether your brand is cited in AI-generated answers and whether visitors are arriving from Gemini, Perplexity, or ChatGPT.

### Examples of Tools Already Positioning Smartly

Some players in the B2B and ecommerce ecosystem are already adapting to the reality of AI-driven discovery.

Shopify, for instance, has rolled out tools like Shopify Magic and Sidekick that help merchants generate AI-ready content. The byproduct? A flood of structured FAQs, store guides, and product copy that AI search engines can easily quote.

BigCommerce

Algolia and Coveo are embedding generative answering directly into their on-site search ecosystems. By doing so, they’re not only serving customers but also training their environments to align with the way AI engines interpret content.

These aren’t just product upgrades. They’re deliberate moves to ensure their ecosystems are AI-friendly and their brands are consistently cited.

### The Right SEO Checklist

Ranking number one on Google

Content without clarity and structure will sink, no matter how polished it looks. Tools that don’t make it into AI summaries risk becoming invisible at the very moment decisions are being made.

The right SEO now involves AEO as well. Keep your SEO fundamentals strong because backlinks, domain authority, and quality content still matter to convince Google. But layer on AEO with answer-focused pages, structured explanations, and comparison-driven insights.

At the same time, push your presence into professional ecosystems like LinkedIn and trusted communities where real conversations happen. And measure success differently, like look at your visibility in AI-generated answers and the quality of traffic arriving from them.

### The New Marketing Imperative

AI tools

It’s not idle curiosity. It signals three things:

1. Trust in practitioners

2. Community Recommendation

3. Purchase proximity:

AI search is becoming the default way B2B buyers discover solutions. For ecommerce tools and platforms, that means facing a choice.

You can stick with old SEO tactics alone and risk being invisible in AI-driven summaries. Or you can embrace the change and expand your visibility across professional ecosystems, ensuring that when buyers ask, “Which tool should I use?” your name appears in the shortlist.

B2B, it’s not about clicks and free trials anymore. It’s about shaping the shortlists that AI puts in front of decision-makers. And shortlists, as every sales team knows, are everything.

### Key Takeaways

AI search is changing how B2B buyers find and trust tools.

Ranking #1 on Google is no longer enough — you need to be cited by AI.

Create clear, structured, and verifiable content so AI can reference you.

LinkedIn and other professional communities are now important citation sources.

Track new metrics like AI citations and referrals, not just keyword rankings.

If you’re not in AI-generated answers, you’re not on the buyer’s shortlist.

## 15 Tried & Tested AI Tools for Ecommerce Success in 2025

Jack Mathew

Updated on Jul 8, 2025

The fastest-growing ecommerce brands today have one thing in common: they’ve made AI the backbone of their speed, personalization, and operational intelligence.

From speeding up product launches to fine-tuning listings and dialing in personalized customer experiences, AI is helping modern brands move faster and smarter, and thus convert better.

And with the retail AI market expected to grow over 30% annually through 2032 (Statista), high-performing ecommerce stores are no longer debating if they should or shouldn’t adopt AI. Instead, they compete in “how well” each store integrates it.

Now the tough question: what tools to pick to make your ecommerce business a success? Over the past two decades, we’ve helped scale everything from lean Shopify startups to enterprise-level rollouts, and in that time, we’ve tried our fair share of AI tools. Many promise the moon; few deliver. So, we have compiled the list of tools that are the true game changers.

Let’s break them down by what ecommerce business owners actually need, starting with visibility.

### AI Tools for SEO & Product Listing

SEO is one of the most tiring and crucial steps in ecommerce success, and rightfully so. If no one can find your products, they won’t buy them.

Search engine optimization is still one of the most cost-effective ways to attract qualified traffic to your store, but doing it well (and at scale) can eat up serious time.

However, the following AI tools can help you speed things up without cutting corners. They can help you rank, write faster, and keep your listings consistent across hundreds or thousands of SKUs.

#### 1. Describely: To Scale Product Descriptions

For ecommerce stores with hundreds or thousands of products, it’s nearly a nightmare to write and update so many unique descriptions.

However, Describely can help you craft them in bulk, and they’ll not only sound like your brand but also play nicely with search engines (SEO Optimized). Moreover, this tool flexes its 98% accuracy rate at first generation, so you know you’re not getting some made-up fluff.

Best use cases:

Bulk generation of product descriptions

Maintaining brand voice across extensive inventories

Enhancing SEO performance for product pages

#### 2.CopyMonkey: To Optimize Amazon Listings

CopyMonkey is best for Amazon sellers or ecommerce businesses expanding to Amazon. It uses AI to craft compelling Amazon product listings while focusing on all things important: titles, bullet points, and descriptions that drive conversions.

Amazon

Best use cases:

Creating high-converting Amazon listings

A/B testing different copy variations

Streamlining the listing creation process

#### 3. Searchspring: For Personalized Product Discovery

Searchspring offers AI-driven search and merchandising solutions that generate relevant product recommendations and dynamic content. Since personalization drives engagement and sales, Searchspring tailors the shopping experience to individual customer preferences.

If you are a mid to large-sized ecommerce business, this is one of your best bets.

Best use cases:

Dynamic product recommendations

Personalized merchandising strategies

A/B testing for search and navigation

### AI Tools for Designing & Product Imaging

Good design sells without saying a word! A great product photo or an interactive video often does more than 500 words of copy.

High-quality visuals and engaging video formats are proven to increase time on site and conversion rates. However, getting these impactful creatives and videos means budget, time, and an experienced team of designers.

The other option is to leverage AI tools. With the following AI design tools, you can turn plain product shots into polished visuals or add shoppable videos to your site, without needing a production crew.

#### 1. Pebblely: For Product Imagery

It’s almost viral among Shopify brands and lean marketing teams.

Pebblely uses AI to transform basic product images into diverse, high-quality visuals suitable for various marketing channels.

Consistent and engaging product imagery is crucial for capturing customer attention, and Pebblely enables brands to produce a variety of visuals without the need for costly photoshoots.

Best use cases:

Generating lifestyle images for marketing campaigns

Creating seasonal or themed product visuals

Enhancing product pages with diverse images

#### 2. Claid.AI: For AI Powered Product Photography

If you’ve ever dealt with the cost, time, and headache of organizing a product photoshoot, you’ll appreciate what Booth.ai brings to the table.

It saves you from booking a studio or juggling with freelancers because all you have to do is upload a reference image and type in a prompt about what kind of shot you're going for. It generates high-quality, aesthetically pleasing product photos that look professionally done.

This can be a game-changer for a new DTC brand trying to keep overhead costs low because Claid has a free monthly package (50 credits per month) as well.

Best use cases:

Rapid generation of product images for new launches

Creating consistent visuals across product lines

Reducing costs associated with traditional photography

#### 3. Tolstoy For Interactive Shoppable Videos

Tolstoy is a great tool for brands targeting younger demographics. It allows brands to create interactive, shoppable videos that enhance customer engagement and product discovery.

Best use cases:

Showcasing product features through video

Creating interactive shopping experiences

Collecting viewer data for personalization

### AI Tools for Customer Support

With time, customers have grown impatient. Every minute you don’t answer a question, a customer bounces.

Therefore, fast and accurate customer support can make or break your conversion rate. A small team of customer support can’t man every channel 24/7, but with the following AI-powered support tools, you can manage inquiries, automate FAQs, and maintain a consistent tone across platforms, 24/7.

#### 1. Gorgias AI: To Enhance Customer Support

Gorgias AI integrates with your ecommerce platform to provide automated, personalized customer support across multiple channels.

Best use cases:

Automating responses to common customer queries

Integrating support across email, chat, and social media

Providing personalized support experiences

#### 2. Tidio Lyro: AI Chatbot for Ecommerce

Tidio Lyro provides an AI-powered chatbot that assists customers in real-time, handling inquiries and guiding them through the shopping process. It’s a great addition for small to medium-sized ecommerce stores, as per our experience.

Best use cases:

Answering product-related questions instantly

Assisting with order tracking and returns

Collecting customer feedback

#### 3. Zendesk AI: For Scalable Customer Support

Zendesk AI is another great option that helps you integrate advanced AI capabilities into customer support to automate responses and provide insights for support teams.

Best use cases:

Automating ticket routing and responses

Analyzing support data for continuous improvement

Integrating support across multiple channels

### AI Tools for Personalization & Conversions

It has been a long time since generic shopping experiences are dead.

Now, customers expect personalized journeys. You have to offer them tailored product recommendations, search results, and post-purchase offers. Sounds like a lot of work?

However, these tools below use AI to turn behavioral data into higher conversion rates and AOV (average order value), so your efforts don’t go in vain.

#### 1. Klevu: For Intelligent Site Search

If you have ecommerce sites with extensive product catalogs, Klevu can enhance on-site search functionality using AI, delivering personalized and relevant search results to your shoppers. Since it provides effective search functionality, it automatically improves user experience and conversion rates because Klevu ensures customers find what they're looking for quickly.

Best use cases:

Implementing personalized search experiences

Analyzing search data for merchandising insights

Enhancing product discovery on-site

### AI Tools for Analytics Forcasting

Inventory decisions shouldn’t be guesswork because your cash is tied up in your inventory. Overstocking kills margins, and understocking kills customer trust.

However, with AI tools, you can predict what will sell, when to reorder, and how to balance supply with demand.

#### 1. Inventory Planner: For Smart Inventory Forecasting

Inventory Planner employs AI to forecast demand, helping businesses maintain optimal stock levels and reduce overstock or stockouts, making it ideal for retail sellers and wholesalers.

Best use cases:

Predicting sales trends for inventory planning

Automating purchase orders

Reducing excess inventory and associated costs

### AI Tools for Marketing & Retargeting

Marketing is getting more expensive, but you can make things work with AI.

To cut through the noise, you need creative that converts and targeting that doesn’t waste ad budget. The following AI tools can help you launch better ads, retarget smarter, and squeeze more ROI out of your budget.

#### 1. AdCreative.AI: For Automated Ad Creation

Effective ad creatives are crucial for campaign success. AdCreative.ai uses AI to generate high-performing ad creatives tailored to your brand and audience. It streamlines the creation process so you save time and resources and spend them on other important tasks.

Best use cases:

Generating ad creatives for multiple platforms

A/B testing different ad variations

Scaling ad production efficiently

#### 2. Pencil: For AI-Powered Video Ads

Pencil is another AI tool you can use to create compelling video ads and analyze performance data to optimize future content. Video ads are increasingly important in digital marketing, and with this tool, you can ensure your video content is both engaging enough to be effective.

Best for:

Brands focusing on video marketing

Top use cases:

Producing data-driven video ads

Testing different video narratives

Enhancing video ad ROI

#### 3. Enhencer: For AI-Driven Audience Targeting

Top use cases:

Segmenting audiences based on behavior

Optimizing ad campaigns for conversions

Reducing customer acquisition costs

#### 4. ReConvert: To Upsell Post-Purchase

Shopify

So, it’s best to maximize revenue from existing customers, which is cost-effective as well.

Best use cases:

Implementing a thank-you page offers

Creating personalized upsell funnels

Tracking post-purchase conversion metrics

### Key Takeaways:

For SEO and product listing, AI tools like Describely and CopyMonkey help you create optimized product listings at scale, which is crucial when you’re managing hundreds (or thousands) of SKUs.

Tools like Pebblely, Booth.AI, and Tolstoy make it easy to create professional images and interactive videos without a design team or costly shoots.

AI tools like Gorgias, Tidio, and Zendesk automate FAQs and streamline customer service so buyers get answers fast.

Tools like Klevu, Searchspring, and ReConvert let you personalize every step, from search to post-purchase upsells for higher conversion and retention.;

Inventory Planner helps predict what will sell and when, so you avoid overstocking or selling out too soon.

With AdCreative.AI, Pencil, and Enhencer, you can launch, test, and optimize high-converting campaigns without burning your ad budget.

## How to Use Chatbots to Automate Customer Experience

Jack Mathew

Updated on Jun 16, 2025

Customers expect instant, 24/7 support, or else you are the “bad guy.” Moreover, they want quick response in natural language at any hour.

However, building a human support team large enough to meet that expectation is not a viable solution for every business, and it's a fast track to a drained budget and a stretched ops team.

But with generative AI in the picture now, you have a solution that keeps your customer experience sharp, personal, and always-on without adding to payroll.

Let’s walk you through the process of using chatbots to automate and improve your customer experience.

### 1. Understand What Chatbots Can Do

Before implementing a chatbot, it’s important to understand what problems it can solve for your business. A good chatbot, even a basic one, can:

Provide 24/7 instant responses to FAQs.

Help customers navigate your site or products.

Collect names, emails, preferences, and even feedback.

Handle routine tasks like appointment scheduling or order tracking.

Qualify leads so your sales team isn’t wasting time on “just browsing” folks.

Understanding these use cases will help clarify if automation will genuinely solve your customer service bottlenecks or not. For instance, bots are great for repetitive stuff. So, if something is asked 20 times a day by 20 different customers, then that’s your green light.

### 2. Identify Key Customer Touchpoints to Automate First

Now, don’t try to automate your entire customer service desk overnight. Start with the low-hanging fruit. So, focus on areas of highest traffic and lowest complexity, such as:

Shipping and return questions.

“Where’s my package?” inquires.

Basic product/service queries.

Store hours, contact details, or location info.

These problems are ideal for automation as they don’t need emotion or complex logic, and when you offload them, your human support team can focus on what really needs their (human) supervision.

### 3. Choose the Right Platform or Chatbot Solution

ecommerce

Tidio (great for ecommerce)

Intercom and Drift (solid for SaaS and B2B)

ManyChat and Chatfuel (if you’re on Messenger or Instagram).

Also when you're choosing the right chatbot platform, the first thing to check is whether it plays nicely with your current website or CRM. And if you serve international customers, make sure the tool also supports multilingual interactions.

Many platforms let you start small with a simple rules-based chatbot (those that follow decision trees), which is a great starting point for beginners. Later, as your needs grow or your traffic increases, you can level up to AI-powered bots like those built on ChatGPT or Dialogflow.

### 4. Train the Bot to Match Your Brand Tone & Workflow

Don’t just install it—train it!

It’s one thing to reply to a question. It’s another to proactively help someone decide. Let’s say a customer types, “Do you have jackets for winter hiking?” A basic chatbot might just say, “Yes, check our jacket collection.”

However, a trained bot with product data can go further: “Yes, we have 12 options for men’s insulated jackets under $200. Would you like me to show options sorted by warmth or waterproofing?”

This makes your bot sound like personal shopping assistants and help minimize decision fatigue of buyers, leading them closer to checkout without pushing them too hard.

Now, how to do it?

Write scripts using your brand voice.

Use casual, short, human-like responses.

Use contractions (“you’re” not “you are”).

Anticipate follow-up questions and build logical flows.

And don’t “set it and forget it”. Regularly check your chat logs. If customers are confused or stuck, tweak the flows.

### 5. Use Chatbots to Collect Data, Not Just Answer

Chatbots shouldn't just be treated as digital assistants for answering FAQs. You can use them strategically to collect rich customer data, like preferences, pain points, and purchase intent, turning every interaction into a learning opportunity for your business.

They can help you gather valuable customer insights like:

Most common queries (useful for improving UX or marketing copy).

Lead info (name, email, needs).

Feedback after a support session.

Use this data to refine your product, update your website content, or hand over qualified leads to the sales team.

### 6. Plan Human Handoff for Complex Queries

Even the best chatbot can’t solve everything. They all have their limits. For instance, when conversations get too complex or sensitive, it’s crucial to have a clear path for handing things off to a real human, ideally. That too, without making the customer repeat themselves or start over. So, make sure you:

Set up smooth human handoff options.

Add a “Talk to a real person” or “Talk to a live agent” option.

Set triggers for bot fallback (e.g., if the question repeats or uses words like "angry" or "complaint").

Amazon

### 7. Monitor, Test, and Improve

Finally, no automation strategy is complete without optimization. So:

Track chatbot performance (open rate, resolution rate, drop-off points).

Use A/B testing for different scripts.

Regularly update responses based on customer behavior or seasonal trends.

Chatbots are not just digital helpers—they’re part of your brand experience. They reduce support costs, increase customer satisfaction, and even boost conversions, but only if used smartly.

reach out to us

You hereby agree to liquidated damages of US $100,000.00 plus costs and actual damages for violating this provision.

DISCLAIMER FOR CONTENTS OF SITE

The website disclaims any responsibility for the accuracy of the content of this website. Visitors assume the all risk of viewing, reading, using, or relying upon this information. Unless you have otherwise formed an express contract to the contrary with the website, you have no right to rely on any information contained herein as accurate. The website makes no such warranty.

DISCLAIMER FOR HARM CAUSED TO YOUR COMPUTER OR SOFTWARE FROM INTERACTING WITH THIS WEBSITE OR ITS CONTENTS. VISITOR ASSUMES ALL RISK OF VIRUSES, WORMS, OR OTHER CORRUPTING FACTORS.

DISCLAIMER FOR HARM CAUSED BY DOWNLOADS

Visitor downloads information from this site at this own risk. Website makes no warranty that downloads are free of corrupting computer codes, including, but not limited to, viruses and worms.

By viewing, using, or interacting in any manner with this site, including banners, advertising, or pop-ups, downloads, and as a condition of the website to allow his lawful viewing, Visitor forever waives all right to claims of damage of any and all description based on any causal factor resulting in any possible harm, no matter how heinous or extensive, whether physical or emotional, foreseeable or unforeseeable, whether personal or business in nature.

All submissions, including portions thereof, graphics contained thereon, or any of the content of the submission, shall become the exclusive property of the Website and may be used, without further permission, for commercial use without additional consideration of any kind. Visitor agrees to only communicate that information to the Website, which it wishes to forever allow the Website to use in any manner as it sees fit. “Submissions” is also a provision of the Privacy Policy.

NOTICE

No additional notice of any kind for any reason is due Visitor and Visitor expressly warrants an understanding that the right to notice is waived as a condition for permission to view or interact with the website.

DISPUTES

As part of the consideration that the Website requires for viewing, using or interacting with this website, Visitor agrees to use binding arbitration for any claim, dispute, or controversy (“CLAIM”) of any kind (whether in contract, tort or otherwise) arising out of or relating to this purchase, this product, including solicitation issues, privacy issues, and terms of use issues.

Information about the American Arbitration Association, its rules, and its forms are available from the American Arbitration Association, 335 Madison Avenue, Floor 10, New York, New York, 10017-4605. In no case shall the viewer, visitor, member, subscriber or customer have the right to go to court or have a jury trial.
